([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--21a4bceed694fba4))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when interpreting ICA metagenes (sk vectors) from cancer omics data: you have a ranked gene list per component and need GO/KEGG functional enrichment to name the biological signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--6ad7eca835c78c8a))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when analyzing PICRUSt2 KO/predicted functional abundance tables and pathway-set (KEGG pathway) level enrichment signals are more appropriate than single-feature differential abundance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--0b9c684e2f8b21fb))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a researcher has a differential gene expression table (e.g. DESeq2 output with gene id, log2 fold change, adjusted p-value) and needs to identify enriched GO terms or KEGG pathways without writing R code. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--ca662a20c20bbe5a))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a selected gene list (e.g. RMT signal genes from Zeisel mouse cortex or Kang PBMC data) plus a discarded list, and need to test over-representation of biological gene sets against the full gene universe. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--b40e6f65893deae2))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have CMScaller-classified (CMS1–CMS4) organoid, PDX or cell-line expression data and need to confirm the subtypes recapitulate known CMS biology (CMS2 HNF4A/MYC/WNT, CMS3 CDX2/metabolic, CMS4 TGFβ/EMT) via gene set enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--cdc66e578a123814))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a list of differentially expressed genes (from a text list, microarray study, or clustered Seurat single-cell object) and want to find statistically enriched gene modules linked to environmental factors such as cigarette smoking, diet, infections, or toxic chemicals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--62e11b3fca63b877))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a list of mouse gene sets (e.g., co-expression modules or GEPs from SingleCellGGM) and need to test which GO or MP terms are statistically enriched at p &lt;= 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--f627d74f6a803298))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have per-factor gene rankings from a trained multi-omics factor model (e.g., Mowgli on TEA-seq) and need to interpret latent factors biologically by testing their top genes for enrichment against curated gene set databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--720b3263feee28da))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a list of gene symbols — especially one recovered from Excel date-conversion errors (Mar-01, Mar-02) — must be functionally annotated via GO Biological Processes enrichment, with terms filtered at adjusted p &lt; 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--15ad35ae14c03fa5))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have differential expression results from the curatedAdipoArray 3T3-L1 microarray data (e.g., Pparg knockdown) and need to rank all genes by decreasing log fold-change to identify significantly enriched GO terms via GSEA rather than overlap-based ORA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--32a1714276960791))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a list of discriminatively selected marker genes needs functional/annotation validation against a curated tissue and cell-type gene-set library such as HuBMAP ASCT+B, or when comparing marker-selection methods by enrichment q values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--d353ddea287c38ac))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have ranked gene-level differential expression statistics (e.g., edgeR log2-fold-changes between two cell clones or conditions) and want to know which MSigDB Hallmark pathways are enriched at FDR &lt; 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--b9da75b08e658f35))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq cluster (e.g., a Leiden cluster from Cellar) needs a cell-type label and differential expression has yielded ranked genes that must be interpreted against GO, KEGG, or MSigDB functional gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--a942e0b359299e26))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a ranked gene list (e.g. FDR-ranked conditionTest DE genes from a multi-condition trajectory analysis) and need to interpret which biological pathways explain between-condition differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--1f9beeabb1328d9f))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a DMC (differentially methylated CpG) list from a 450K/EPIC Illumina array analysis needs to be translated into enriched biological pathways with per-database enrichment statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis--861e9eec3094864e))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when when you have a ranked list of gene-level statistics (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-set-enrichment-analysis))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when performing ranked gene-set enrichment after differential expression, including identifier mapping, deterministic rank construction, gene-set versioning, multilevel enrichment, leading-edge review, and bounded pathway interpretation. Do not rank only significant genes. ([Linxira-OS/linxira-skills](https://github.com/Linxira-OS/linxira-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-set-enrichment-analysis))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis-gsea**: Use when genes ranked by an inferred latent process (e.g., Cyclum circular pseudotime match scores, or PCA components 1-2) must be validated against a curated gene set such as GO:0007049 to test whether the ranking captures cell-cycle biology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis-gsea))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis-hallmark-pathways**: Use when scMDC cluster assignments for a CITE-seq dataset (e.g., BMNC GSE128639) exist and you need per-cluster Hallmark pathway enrichment from differential gene rankings to interpret cluster biology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis-hallmark-pathways))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis-interpretation**: Use when a gene list of temporal DEGs from multi-timepoint scRNA-seq (e.g., CASi tDEGs at p &lt; 0.05) needs pathway-level interpretation (Hallmark/Reactome) and key-gene trajectory visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis-interpretation))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis-of-cluster-markers**: Use when you have Seurat FindAllMarkers output (only.pos=TRUE, min.pct=0.25, logfc.threshold=0.25) for scRNA-seq clusters and need reference-database cell-type annotations that respect gene ranking rather than simple list overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis-of-cluster-markers))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis-scrnaseq**: Use when you have gene-level differential expression summary statistics (beta, beta_var or p-values) from scRNA-seq data (e.g. MAST, SCDE, zingeR-DESeq2) and need calibrated gene set enrichment p-values and FDR estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis-scrnaseq))
- **gene-set-enrichment-analysis-with-comet**: Use when SCA (QCM/QCC &gt; 0.5) has identified supported clusters and you must functionally annotate their cluster-specific genes against immune-signature or KEGG gene sets to validate cell identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-analysis-with-comet))
- **gene-set-enrichment-annotation-scoring**: Use when cluster-level marker tables (gene symbol, avg_log2FC per Seurat cluster) must be converted into per-cluster cell-type recommendations, and when comparing GSEA vs Fisher exact test accuracy across marker databases (CellMarker, PanglaoDB, Clustermole). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-annotation-scoring))
- **gene-set-enrichment-auc-analysis**: Use when projecting leukemia cell sub-clusters onto a reference hematopoietic atlas and you need the gene-set-enrichment AUC-difference component of the CCI composite score to measure similarity between compared sub-clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-auc-analysis))
- **gene-set-enrichment-auc-scoring**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data has been clustered into CD8 TIL states and you need quantitative per-cell scores for phenotype axes defined by marker gene sets (e.g. Pdcd1/Havcr2 exhaustion, Gzmb cytotoxicity, Tcf7 stemness). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-auc-scoring))
- **gene-set-enrichment-based-cell-type-annotation**: Use when you have a Seurat object with clusters and a per-cluster positive marker list (e.g. FindAllMarkers output mapped to Entrez IDs) and need automatic, ranking-aware cell-type annotation instead of manual marker matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-based-cell-type-annotation))
- **gene-set-enrichment-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a directed/undirected subnetwork extraction method must be quantitatively compared against active-subnetwork alternatives on a case-vs-control RNA-seq benchmark with known disease genes and GO term references. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-benchmark-evaluation))
- **gene-set-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when you have a BED list of ChIP-seq peaks (e.g. bivalent H3K4me3+/H3K27me3+ peaks in mESCs) and need to identify enriched biological functions such as developmental GO categories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-interpretation))
- **gene-set-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when scRNA-seq topics/embeddings must be interpreted against known biological pathways, or when gene set/pathway knowledge should be directly embedded into a topic model so pathway-topic associations are learned rather than inferred post hoc. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-interpretation))
- **gene-set-enrichment-of-dynamic-genes**: Use when scLANE (or a similar trajectory DE tool) has called genes significantly dynamic along a Slingshot/Monocle3 trajectory at FDR-adjusted p &lt; 0.01, and you need pathway-level interpretation of lineage-specific dynamics such as osteoblastic vs. apoptotic fates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-of-dynamic-genes))
- **gene-set-enrichment-pvalue-calibration-assessment**: Use when benchmarking GSE methods (e.g., iDEA, fGSEA, PAGE, GSEA, CAMERA) on scRNA-seq data and you must verify null p-value calibration rather than power, especially across varying gene set coverage rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-pvalue-calibration-assessment))
- **gene-set-enrichment-score-computation**: Use when an AnnData object processed via the scanpy standard workflow (log-cpm normalised, high-resolution Leiden labels) needs per-cluster enrichment scores to assign cells to marker-defined clusters, or when comparing which scoring method discriminates clusters defined by unique gene combinations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-score-computation))
- **gene-set-enrichment-score-ranking**: Use when the goal is to rank predefined gene-sets (e.g., KEGG or Biocarta pathways) by enrichment score in a two-group comparison and assess their significance by random label permutation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-score-ranking))
- **gene-set-enrichment-scoring**: Use when annotating clusters in a Seurat scRNA-seq object against a heterogeneous catalog of variable-sized cell-type marker gene sets, and you need bias-corrected per-cluster enrichment scores with statistical significance calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-scoring--f84800f41666473c))
- **gene-set-enrichment-scoring**: Use when raw gene expression profiles (rows = samples, columns = Entrez gene identifiers) must be transformed into pathway activity features — e.g., to train or apply a DeepCC subtype classifier, or to make batch/platform-robust single-sample predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-scoring--8604bb15b1aa2ee0))
- **gene-set-enrichment-scoring-coregulation**: Use when you have a normalized gene expression matrix (log2-quantile normalized, filtered to high-variance genes) and a collection of annotated gene sets (e.g., Reactome, MSigDB pathways), and need to test whether specific pathways show significant coordinated expression shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/gene-set-enrichment-scoring-coregulation))
- **gene-set-enrichment-spectra-computation**: Use when converting raw gene expression matrices (rows=samples, columns=Entrez gene IDs) into pathway-activity features using MSigDB gene sets, prior to training a DeepCC subtype classifier or classifying new/individual samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-spectra-computation))
- **gene-set-enrichment-statistics**: Use when a gene list derived from a co-expression module (e.g., a PyWGCNA module correlated with an external trait at P &lt; 0.05) needs GO/KEGG/REACTOME functional enrichment to identify the biological functions the module represents. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-statistics))
- **gene-set-enrichment-summary-statistics-testing**: Use when you have per-gene DE summary statistics (e.g., z-scores from MAST/SCDE/zingeR) plus gene set annotations and need a calibrated p-value testing whether each gene set is enriched in differentially expressed genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-summary-statistics-testing))
- **gene-set-enrichment-testing-via-fishers-exact-test**: Use when a fitted topic model (e.g., CODAL/MIRA β matrix) yields a ranked gene list and you need to test overrepresentation of a curated cell-type ontology set — e.g., HuBMAP CD16+ monocyte membership — among the top-ranked genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-testing-via-fishers-exact-test))
- **gene-set-enrichment-validation-with-vam**: Use when a rare, newly recovered single-cell subcluster must be confirmed against a published marker gene set — e.g., an apCAF subcluster (23 cells, 0.092% of the Elyada PDAC dataset) that was absent from the original analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-enrichment-validation-with-vam))
- **gene-set-fetch**: Fetches canonical named gene sets and ad-hoc parameterized gene sets (human, mouse), writing provenance-stamped TSV + meta JSON. Named: transcription factors (Lambert 2018, AnimalTFDB, JASPAR motif-backed), protein-coding (Ensembl). Ad-hoc: disease-gene associations via Open Targets (default), GWAS Catalog, or OMIM; genomic structural features (head-to-head / bidirectional promoters via Ensembl BioMart). Use for "list of genes in category X" requests — TFs, protein-coding, "genes associated with disease X", "head-to-head pairs on chr Y". Distinct from gget (gene-centric lookups) and gene-anno… ([ppavlidis/skillz](https://github.com/ppavlidis/skillz/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-set-fetch))
- **gene-set-file-construction**: Use when preparing seed, source, or target gene sets as input files for RWRtoolkit functions (RWR_LOE, RWR_ShortestPaths, RWR_CV) against a multiplex network, and genes are given as AGI identifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-set-file-construction))
- **Gene-Set-Foundation-Model**: Query the Gene Set Foundation Model (GSFM) website for gene function predictions, run live GSFM inference on custom gene sets, and run GSFM-based GSEA enrichment. Use when a user asks about predicted gene functions/associations, wants to look up what a model predicts for a gene, wants to submit a custom gene list for augmentation/inference, or wants to run enrichment analysis against gene set libraries using GSFM. ([MaayanLab/maayan-lab-tools-skills](https://github.com/MaayanLab/maayan-lab-tools-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/Gene-Set-Foundation-Model) · ★7)
- **gene-set-intersection-analysis**: Use when the input is a disease-gene association table (e.g. GutUDB DISEASES module) and the goal is to identify which genes are shared across two or more intestinal diseases, or to tally per-disease gene counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-set-intersection-analysis))
- **gene-set-mask-construction**: Use when training TOSICA on a reference AnnData and you must pick or construct the gmt-path mask (human_gobp, human_immune, human_reactome, human_tf, mouse_gobp, mouse_reactome, mouse_tf, or a custom .gmt path) that defines gene-to-pathway-token connectivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-mask-construction))
- **gene-set-module-score-computation**: Use when the goal is to convert annotated gene sets (pathways) into continuous per-spot or per-cell activity features for downstream KLD-based differential-activity testing, e.g., on Visium spatial transcriptomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-module-score-computation))
- **gene-set-outlier-thresholding**: Use when computing PC1/SVD-based gene set (module) activity from a normalized expression matrix and you must remove aberrant genes that would distort the percentage of variance explained by PC1 (L1) before scoring module activity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-outlier-thresholding))
- **gene-set-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have two or more differential-expression-derived gene lists (e.g., H-NS regulons from GSE136050 RNA-seq and a published 308-gene LuxR regulon) and need to compute and validate their overlap count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-overlap-analysis))
- **gene-set-overlap-analysis**: Use when comparing a ranked or selected gene list against published biomarker signatures or DEG lists (e.g., top-14 UGCB sets) to report overlap counts, percentages, and shared gene symbols. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-overlap-analysis))
- **gene-set-overlap-enrichment-analysis**: Use when two gene sets from independent studies (e.g. metabolite-interacting genes and GWAS trait genes) must be statistically compared for over-represented overlap via a hypergeometric upper-tail test. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-set-overlap-enrichment-analysis))
- **gene-set-overlap-jaccard-analysis**: Use when comparing significant gene-set or DE gene call lists from two or more enrichment/DE methods and you need a set-level concordance metric to confirm that a more method's extra hits are consistent with existing calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-overlap-jaccard-analysis))
- **gene-set-overlap-quantification**: Use when auditing whether a published PDAC signature (Bailey ADEX, Exocrine, Immunogenic) is confounded by non-tumor-specific genes, or when you need to reproduce signature-vs-signature overlap fractions across curated pdacR bulk RNAseq datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-overlap-quantification))
- **gene-set-overlap-rate-computation**: Use when evaluating whether a feature-selection strategy (Seurat-style HVG, random-forest HIG, or scCAD ensemble) preserves a reference rare-cell-type DE gene set, using OR1/OR2/OR3 overlap rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-overlap-rate-computation))
- **gene-set-overrepresentation-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a filtered gene list (e.g. markers exceeding an AUC threshold) and need to test whether defined gene sets (GO CC/MF terms) are overrepresented at FDR &lt; 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-overrepresentation-enrichment-analysis))
- **gene-set-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have JOINTLY factor/module gene sets (liver$modules) or factor-associated genes and need to know which biological processes or pathways each interpretable factor represents. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-pathway-enrichment-analysis--eb2bfbbdd7b530dc))
- **gene-set-pathway-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a single-cell GEX matrix has already been QC-filtered, clustered, and cell-type classified within the gExcite/scAmpi workflow, and differentially expressed genes per cluster must be assigned biological pathway context. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-pathway-enrichment-analysis--ccdfe37b15c2e1ca))
- **gene-set-pca-activity-scoring**: Use when you have a normalized gene-by-sample expression matrix (RNA-seq cpm/vst or proteomics) plus gene sets (GMT/MSigDB or custom signatures) and need per-sample module activity scores with statistical significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-pca-activity-scoring))
- **gene-set-permutation-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantitatively validate a gene-set-scoring cell-type annotation pipeline — i.e., measure how annotation accuracy (sensitivity/specificity) degrades as marker sets are corrupted with random genes at increasing replacement rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-permutation-benchmarking))
- **gene-set-perturbation-analysis**: Use when RNA-seq expression data with case/control group labels (e.g. GEO GSE172114) must be mapped onto extracted network submodules and compared for perturbation strength via source-sink path ACEs and Bonferroni-combined P-values plus DEG counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-perturbation-analysis))
- **gene-set-score-computation**: Use when building or applying a ProjectSVR reference model: you need a gene-set (signature) score matrix — e.g., from cNMF-derived or overexpression-tested gene sets, top 25 genes per set — as regression input features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-score-computation))
- **gene-set-score-computation-across-bulk-transcriptomic-datasets**: Use when you have bulk genes-by-samples expression matrices (microarray or RNAseq, e.g. pdacR $ex objects) with sample-level tissue metadata, and you need per-sample gene-set scores to compare signature activity across compartments like normal pancreas, primary PDAC, PDX, CAF lines, and metastases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-score-computation-across-bulk-transcriptomic-datasets))
- **gene-set-score-feature-augmentation**: Use when preparing representative single-cell reference data for scSemiProfiler's deep generative model, where raw normalized expression alone under-constrains in silico inference of target samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-score-feature-augmentation))
- **gene-set-size-filtering**: Use when preparing gene-by-gene-set annotation matrices (e.g. binary 0/1 data.frames from MSigDB or MGI GO GAF 2.0 files) as inputs for iDEA GSE analysis, and sparse sets below the size threshold must be removed first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-size-filtering))
- **gene-set-size-null-model-fitting**: Use when scoring cell-type marker gene sets of widely varying sizes against scRNA-seq clusters (e.g., Miko scoring on a Cell Catalog or permuted marker sets) and raw enrichment scores must be corrected for gene set-size bias before significance calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-size-null-model-fitting))
- **gene-set-size-stratified-summary-statistics**: Use when comparing gene-set scoring methods across multiple BP sets and you need overall plus Ngenes-stratified (e.g., Ngenes&gt;200) summary statistics such as mean Pearson correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-size-stratified-summary-statistics))
- **gene-set-spatial-relevance-benchmarking**: Use when validating a pathway activity scoring method's ability to detect spatially relevant gene sets, by benchmarking delta-KLD separation between true and random gene sets across simulated ST spatial organization patterns (hotspot, gradient, streak). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-spatial-relevance-benchmarking))
- **gene-set-specificity-scoring-jsd**: Use when you already have per-cell pathway activity weights (e.g. from run.rwr on an MCA co-embedding) and spot/cell group annotations, and need a numeric JSD-based score of how specific each gene set is to a cluster or anatomical annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-specificity-scoring-jsd))
- **gene-set-submatrix-decomposition**: Use when raw gene-expression data must be translated into per-sample, per-pathway activity levels using predefined KEGG/Biocarta gene sets before group comparisons such as diabetic vs. non-diabetic or smoker vs. non-smoker. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-set-submatrix-decomposition))
- **gene-set-tissue-specificity-quantification**: Use when a multi-tissue differential expression study (e.g., MoTrPAC PASS1B rat endurance training, 15 tissues) needs a quantitative measure of DE gene-set overlap and tissue-specificity across tissues. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gene-set-tissue-specificity-quantification))
- **gene-set-transcription-factor-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have gene programs (e.g., MetaTiME MeCs) with gene-level weights and want to identify putative upstream TF regulators by intersecting TF binding/potential scores with the program's TF weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-transcription-factor-enrichment-analysis))
- **gene-set-variation-analysis-scoring**: Use when you have a raw RNA-seq counts matrix (Ensembl IDs, .csv) and want gene-set-level activity scores (e.g. hypoxia/glycolysis) per sample to enable group comparisons, KM/Cox analysis or correlation of pathway activity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-variation-analysis-scoring--6fda77674f3d0cc5))
- **gene-set-variation-analysis-scoring**: Use when scRNA-seq cells need pathway-level activity scores (e.g. MSigDB angiogenesis or a user GMT gene set) to flag pathway-high subpopulations within a tumor microenvironment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-set-variation-analysis-scoring--033f61e732b23967))
- **gene-sharing-network-generative-model**: Generative model for bipartite gene-sharing networks explaining evolutionary patterns in viruses and mobile genetic elements. Captures scale-free gene degree and exponential genome degree distributions via horizontal gene transfer, gene capture, and loss processes. Activation: gene-sharing network, bipartite network evolution, viral genome evolution, pangenome modeling, horizontal gene transfer. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/vision-generative/gene-sharing-network-generative-model) · ★4)
- **gene-signature-aggregate-scoring**: Use when you have a gene set (TMGs/NMGs or other DEG-derived signature) and a matched expression-plus-phenotype matrix (e.g., 962 GDSC cancer cell lines x 265 compound IC50 values) and need a single signature-level score per sample to correlate with the phenotype. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-aggregate-scoring))
- **gene-signature-based-sample-annotation**: Use when bulk PDAC expression samples need subtype calls under one or more published signatures, or when concordance between Moffitt, Collisson, and Bailey subtype labels must be quantified per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-based-sample-annotation))
- **gene-signature-based-training-label-calibration**: Use when building or retraining a malignant-vs-normal scRNA-seq classifier and the initial malignancy labels come from heterogeneous original annotations that may contain mislabeled cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-based-training-label-calibration))
- **gene-signature-enrichment-scoring**: Use when RCC clusters (e.g. CT/PAN glioblastoma clusters or SKCM subtypes) must be validated against a known transcriptional signature to confirm biologically distinct subtype identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-enrichment-scoring))
- **gene-signature-geneset-handling**: Use when assembling gene signatures for UCell/AddModuleScore_UCell scoring — e.g. building Tcell, Myeloid, NK, Plasma_cell marker lists on a scRNA-seq matrix, marking negative markers with '-', or deciding how absent genes should be treated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-geneset-handling))
- **gene-signature-merging**: Use when you have multiple cell-type- or tissue-specific gene signatures (e.g., SenePy hub signatures) and need to derive a consolidated universal or tissue-level signature with statistically filtered, weighted genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-merging))
- **gene-signature-per-cell-scoring**: Use when you have an annotated Seurat object with condition metadata and want per-cell scores and top genes for a pathway signature (e.g., innate immune response) across conditions and cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-per-cell-scoring))
- **gene-signature-reproducibility-checking**: Use when a published nested-CV transcriptomic model reports a final fitted gene signature (ranked nonzero coefficients) and you need to verify your refit retains the same genes in the same order. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-reproducibility-checking))
- **gene-signature-scoring**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq count matrix plus a set of pre-defined gene signatures (.gmt) and want per-cell signature Z-scores and a statistic of whether signature variation tracks a cell similarity structure such as a phylogeny or KNN graph. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-scoring))
- **gene-signature-scoring-for-exhaustion**: Use when a cell population (e.g., 2064 melanoma T cells) has been partitioned into subpopulations (C1–C5) in reconstructed pseudo-space and you must test whether the subpopulation nearest malignant cells carries a higher functional signature score (exhaustion) than a distant one. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-scoring-for-exhaustion))
- **gene-signature-scoring-from-degs**: Use when a single-cell cluster's DEGs (e.g., scanpyobj.h5ad / cluster_DEGs.csv) must be tested against bulk RNA-seq cohorts (e.g., TCGA-LIHC) for prognostic significance via signature scoring and survival analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-scoring-from-degs))
- **gene-signature-scoring-pdac-tumors**: Use when you have a bulk RNA-seq tumor expression matrix plus two competing gene signatures (basal-like vs classical) and need per-sample subtype calls comparable to published schema, followed by survival testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-scoring-pdac-tumors))
- **gene-signature-specificity-partitioning**: Use when a curated gene signature needs to be audited for tissue- or tumor-specificity confounders — e.g., checking if ADEX/Immunogenic genes are driven by non-PDAC or adrenal (ACC) signal before using the signature for subtyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-specificity-partitioning))
- **gene-signature-weight-estimation**: Use when mapping target scRNA-seq cells to reference clusters and you need discriminative weights for cluster-specific gene signatures — e.g. before scid_multiclass scoring or when batch effect/strong asymmetry in cell number and library size means weights should come from the target data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-signature-weight-estimation))
- **gene-significance-filtering**: Use when a CSV of pre-computed log2 fold-changes and adjusted p-values for two treatments vs a common control must be partitioned into significance-filtered regions of interest (quadrants A–E, or M/R/S in the full scorecard) by fold-change magnitude. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-significance-filtering))
- **gene-significance-selection-thresholding**: Use when you have a gene-by-cell expression matrix and a redPATH pseudotime ordering and need to rank or filter genes for significance along the trajectory using dCor and MIC statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-significance-selection-thresholding))
- **gene-similarity-distance-matrix-computation**: Use when preparing the gene-similarity input required by the TACTiCS class in tactics.py: you have protein sequences for the genes of two species' scRNA-seq datasets and need the distance matrix describing gene similarities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-similarity-distance-matrix-computation))
- **gene-similarity-scoring**: Use when two single-cell pseudotime trajectories (e.g., human and mouse bone marrow) have been aligned by CAPITAL tree alignment and you need to score which marker genes show conserved versus species-biased expression kinetics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-similarity-scoring))
- **gene-space-batch-correction-combat-scgen**: Use when evaluating RNA velocity models on multi-batch data and you need gene-space batch-corrected baselines (ComBat or scGen feeding UniTVelo) to compare against dynamics-based correction such as LatentVelo's batch_correction=True VAE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-space-batch-correction-combat-scgen))
- **gene-space-reconstruction-via-domain-specific-batch-normalization**: Use when integrating multi-batch scRNA-seq data (e.g., log1p-normalized pancreas matrix, 2000 HVGs) and you need corrected outputs in the original gene space instead of only latent embeddings, avoiding the interpretability loss of latent-space-only methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-space-reconstruction-via-domain-specific-batch-normalization))
- **gene-specific-allele-frequency-thresholding**: Use when classifying germline SNVs/InDels against ACMG/AMP criteria (BA1/BS1/PM2/PM1/PM4) and gene-specific gnomAD allele-frequency thresholds, not a single global MAF cutoff, are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/gene-specific-allele-frequency-thresholding))
- **gene-specific-bulk-expression-transformation**: Use when decomposing bulk RNA-seq into cell-type proportions using a scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq reference where technical differences between single-cell and bulk chemistries introduce gene-specific biases that break direct proportionality assumptions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-specific-bulk-expression-transformation))
- **gene-specific-latent-time-inference**: Use when the dataset contains complex branching, cycling progenitors, or sparse cell types where a single transcriptome-wide latent time is inappropriate, and per-gene time is needed to recover trajectory structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-specific-latent-time-inference))
- **gene-specific-length-scale-gaussian-process-modeling**: Use when identifying spatially variable genes in spatially-resolved transcriptomics data (e.g., Visium DLPFC, ST mouse OB) where genes may have different spatial ranges (length scales) and scalable fitting over thousands of spots is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-specific-length-scale-gaussian-process-modeling))
- **gene-specific-linear-bias-simulation**: Use when evaluating or benchmarking bulk RNA-seq cell-type decomposition methods under simulated single-cell-vs-bulk technical bias, especially with snRNA-seq reference data such as adipose nuclei. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-specific-linear-bias-simulation))
- **gene-start-annotation-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating gene start prediction accuracy of metagenomic gene finders on complete genomes and you need error counts stratified by the translation initiation signal (canonical RBS, non-canonical RBS, leaderless) identified upstream of reference starts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-start-annotation-benchmarking))
- **gene-statistics**: Per-gene meta-statistics quantifying gene-level biases. v0.1 ships `multifunctionality` — the Gillis & Pavlidis (2011) AUC-style score over GO BP annotations that flags genes likely to appear as top hits in any guilt-by-association / enrichment / DE analysis regardless of the underlying biology. Future: Differential Expression Prior (Crow, Pavlidis, Gillis 2019). Use for MF score, "is this gene always a hit", DE prior, guilt-by-association bias. Distinct from gene-annotations (raw annotations), ontology-terms (structure), gene-set-fetch (catalogs). ([ppavlidis/skillz](https://github.com/ppavlidis/skillz/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-statistics))
- **gene-structure-mapper**: Use gene structure mapper for data analysis workflows that need structured execution, explicit assumptions, and clear output boundaries. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Data%20Analysis/gene-structure-mapper) · ★2.1k · 2 copies)
- **gene-subset-model-retraining**: Use when you must determine whether a reduced or stratified gene feature set (e.g., pan-cancer-common vs. dataset-unique DEGs) retains a trained malignant-vs-normal classifier's performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-subset-model-retraining))
- **gene-subspace-assignment**: Use when a preprocessed scRNA-seq training gene set (genes expressed in at least two cells with at least one percent non-zero entries) must be partitioned into gene subspaces so that dropout-imputation regression can be run within each group. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-subspace-assignment))
- **gene-symbol-cross-referencing**: Use when you have gene symbol lists from a network/biomarker pipeline and need to annotate each gene with drug-actionability status (e.g., DrugBank active targets) and literature association to identify novel candidate biomarkers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-symbol-cross-referencing))
- **gene-symbol-disambiguation-widget-implementation**: Use when building or extending Gene Updater–style date-to-gene conversion and input data contains duplicate Mar-01 or Mar-02 terms that could each map to two genes (MTARC1/MARCHF1, MTARC2/MARCHF2), requiring user-assigned resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-symbol-disambiguation-widget-implementation))
- **gene-symbol-standardization**: Use when combining marker gene sets from multiple published marker databases or cell-sorting collections and you need consistent gene nomenclature and cell type labels before computing cross-source overlaps or integrating markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-symbol-standardization))
- **gene-synteny-cluster-inference-from-contig-adjacency**: Use when you have VAMB clusters.tsv, assembled contigs and reads from a metagenomics run and want to group genes into synteny clusters by contig adjacency, then attach eggNOG-mapper KEGG/GO/Pfam annotations to assess intra-cluster functional agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-synteny-cluster-inference-from-contig-adjacency))
- **gene-taxa-link-analysis**: Use when a case-control gene association test has flagged disease-associated genes (e.g. from KEGG/UniRef90-based gene analysis of SCZ metagenomes) and you need a gene-to-taxon attribution by tracing sequencing reads back to source organisms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-taxa-link-analysis))
- **gene-tf-matrix-construction-with-peak-features**: Use when you have open-chromatin candidate regions (e.g. ATAC/DNase peaks) with activity scores and need a gene-level TF affinity matrix (genes as rows, TFs as columns) whose scaling follows the chosen region–gene mapping mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-tf-matrix-construction-with-peak-features))
- **gene-therapy-plan**: Plan gene therapy development programs. TRIGGERS - Use when user needs help with gene-therapy-plan related tasks. ([lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-therapy-plan) · ★39 · 2 copies)
- **gene-therapy-scientist**: Think and work like an expert Gene Therapy Scientist. Use when a task calls for Gene Therapy Scientist judgment. Reasons from vector biology, biodistribution, linked potency, and integration risk through ddPCR titer, empty/full analytics, ISA and off-target NGS (GUIDE-seq, CIRCLE-seq), and ICH S12 nonclinical design while treating pre-existing capsid neutralizing antibodies, RCV/RCA/RCR positivity, gonadal vector genome, and oligoclonal VCN expansion as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/gene-therapy-scientist/skills/gene-therapy-scientist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **gene-therapy-scientist**: Expert-thinking profile for Gene Therapy Scientist (clinical / research): Reasons from vector biology, biodistribution, linked potency, and integration risk through ddPCR titer, empty/full analytics, ISA and off-target NGS (GUIDE-seq, CIRCLE-seq), and ICH S12 nonclinical design while treating pre-existing capsid neutralizing antibodies, RCV/RCA/RCR positivity, gonadal vector genome, and... ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-therapy-scientist) · ★13)
- **gene-therapy-scientist**: Reasons from vector biology, biodistribution, linked potency, and integration risk through ddPCR titer, empty/full analytics, ISA and off-target NGS (GUIDE-seq, CIRCLE-seq), and ICH S12 nonclinical design while treating pre-existing capsid neutralizing antibodies, RCV/RCA/RCR positivity, gonadal vector genome, and oligoclonal VCN expansion as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/gene-therapy-scientist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **gene-therapy-scientist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for gene therapy scientist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/gene-therapy-scientist))
- **gene-time-correlation-analysis**: Use when ranking genes by Pearson correlation with a continuous time variable (e.g., a 12-hour C. elegans developmental course) and comparing top-N correlation distributions to score denoising recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-time-correlation-analysis))
- **gene-to-care-navigator**: Translates any genetic test report into plain language plus the published care implications, covering surveillance protocols, patient organisations and registries. It does not diagnose or recommend treatment. - **genehmigungspflicht-pruefung**: Prüft, ob ein konkretes Rechtsgeschäft, eine Maßnahme oder eine Entscheidung des Betreuers der Genehmigung des Betreuungsgerichts bedarf (§§ 1848 ff. BGB) — etwa Grundstücksverkauf, Erbausschlagung, Heimvertragsabschluss, Wohnungsauflösung, freiheitsentziehende Maßnahmen. Lädt, wenn Schlagwörter wie "Genehmigung Betreuungsgericht", "§ 1848 BGB", "§ 1850 BGB", "§ 1851 BGB", "freiheitsentziehende Maßnahme" oder "Heimvertrag" auftreten. ([Koftakhor/germanlegal](https://github.com/Koftakhor/germanlegal/tree/HEAD/betreuungsrecht/skills/genehmigungspflicht-pruefung) · ★1)
